   526	export class BrowserBinanceTransport implements TransportSocket {
   527	  readyState = CONNECTING;
   528	  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
   529	  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null = null;
   530	  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null = null;
   531	  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
   532	
   533	  readonly url: string;
   534	  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch;
   535	  private readonly WebSocketImpl: new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket;
   536	  private readonly now: () => number;
   537	  private readonly setTimer: (callback: () => void, ms: number) => unknown;
   538	  private readonly clearTimer: (handle: unknown) => void;
   539	  private readonly endpoints: BrowserBinanceEndpoints;
   540	  private session: Session | null = null;
   541	  private openTimer: unknown = null;
   542	  /** The worker opted in to marker_history frames (its `capabilities` message), as the runtime remembers per client. */
   543	  private markerHistoryOptIn = false;
   544	
   545	  constructor(url: string, options: BrowserBinanceTransportOptions = {}) {
   546	    this.url = url;
   547	    this.fetchImpl = options.fetch ?? ((input, init) => fetch(input, init));
   548	    this.WebSocketImpl = options.WebSocket ?? (WebSocket as unknown as new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket);
   549	    this.now = options.now ?? (() => Date.now());
   550	    this.setTimer = options.setTimeout ?? ((callback, ms) => setTimeout(callback, ms));
   551	    this.clearTimer = options.clearTimeout ?? ((handle) => clearTimeout(handle as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>));
   552	    this.endpoints = { ...DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS, ...(options.endpoints ?? {}) };
   553	    // A WebSocket opens asynchronously; so does this, so the worker installs
   554	    // its handlers before anything fires.
   555	    this.openTimer = this.setTimer(() => {
   556	      this.openTimer = null;
   557	      if (this.readyState !== CONNECTING) return;
   558	      this.readyState = OPEN;
   559	      this.onopen?.(new Event("open"));
   560	      // The runtime pushes its current pair on connect; with no selection
   561	      // from the worker we do the same with the catalog's first spot pair.
   562	      if (this.session === null && this.readyState === OPEN) this.select(defaultSelection());
   563	    }, 0);
   564	  }
   565	
   566	  /** Closed bars held back while a gap fill is pending (diagnostics and tests). */
   567	  get heldClosedBarCount(): number {
   568	    return this.session?.heldClosedBars.size ?? 0;
   569	  }
   570	
   571	  send(data: string): void {
   572	    if (this.readyState !== OPEN) throw new DOMException("transport is not open", "InvalidStateError");
   573	    let msg: unknown;
   574	    try {
   575	      msg = JSON.parse(data);
   576	    } catch {
   577	      return;
   578	    }
   579	    if (!isRecord(msg) || typeof msg.type !== "string") return;
   580	    if (msg.type === "select") {
   581	      const selection = parseSelectRequest(msg);
   582	      if (selection !== null) this.select(selection);
   583	      return;
   584	    }
   585	    if (msg.type === "capabilities") {
   586	      // As the runtime: opting in gets the current marker set at once (nothing
   587	      // when there is none yet; the bootstrap sends it when it completes).
   588	      const optIn = msg.marker_history === true;
   589	      const session = this.session;
   590	      this.markerHistoryOptIn = optIn;
   591	      if (optIn && session !== null) this.setTimer(() => this.deliverMarkerHistory(session), 0);
   592	      return;
   593	    }
   594	    const reply = this.replyFor(msg);
   595	    if (reply !== null) this.setTimer(() => this.deliver(reply), 0);
   596	  }
   597	
   598	  close(): void {
   599	    if (this.readyState === CLOSED) return;
   600	    this.readyState = CLOSED;
   601	    if (this.openTimer !== null) {
   602	      this.clearTimer(this.openTimer);
   603	      this.openTimer = null;
   604	    }
   605	    this.endSession();
   606	  }
   607	
   608	  /** The contract's own "not available" answer for a request this transport cannot serve; null when the contract defines none. */
   609	  private replyFor(msg: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | null {
   610	    const session = this.session;
   611	    if (session === null) return null;
   612	    const identity = identityFields(session.selection);
   613	    const echo = (keys: readonly string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null => {
   614	      const fields: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   615	      for (const key of keys) {
   616	        if (msg[key] === undefined) return null;
   617	        fields[key] = msg[key];
   618	      }
   619	      return fields;
   620	    };
   621	    switch (msg.type) {
   622	      case "aggressive_history":
   623	        return this.aggressiveHistoryReply(session, msg);
   624	      case "delta_profile": {
   625	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "row_width"]);
   626	        if (fields === null) return null;
   627	        return { type: "delta_profile", ...identity, ...fields, complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, pending: 0, rows: [] };
   628	      }
   629	      case "band_history": {
   630	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi", "column_ms"]);
   631	        if (fields === null) return null;
   632	        return { type: "band_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, row_lo: null, row_hi: null, complete: false, reason: "GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, columns: [] };
   633	      }
   634	      case "wall_history": {
   635	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi"]);
   636	        if (fields === null) return null;
   637	        return { type: "wall_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, complete: false, reason: "HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, walls: [], next: null };
   638	      }
   639	      case "l2_event_history": {
   640	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi"]);
   641	        if (fields === null) return null;
   642	        return { type: "l2_event_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, complete: false, reason: "HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, trades: [], wall_trades: [], trades_held_from: null, next: null };
   643	      }
   644	      case "profile_config":
   645	      case "profile_history":
   646	        return { type: "analytics_status", ...identity, indicator: "PROFILE", status: "UNAVAILABLE" };
   647	      default:
   648	        // capabilities, liquidity_resync, l2_resync, exhaustion_history,
   649	        // initiative_history: the contract defines no reply shape, so the
   650	        // indicators keep their own "no frame yet" state.
   651	        return null;
   652	    }
   653	  }
   654	
   655	  private deliver(frame: Record<string, unknown>): void {
   656	    if (this.readyState !== OPEN) return;
   657	    const data = JSON.stringify(frame);
   658	    const event = typeof MessageEvent === "function"
   659	      ? new MessageEvent("message", { data })
   660	      : ({ data } as unknown as MessageEvent);
   661	    this.onmessage?.(event);
   662	  }
   663	
   664	  private endSession(): void {
   665	    const session = this.session;
   666	    if (session === null) return;
   667	    this.session = null;
   668	    session.closed = true;
   669	    session.abort?.abort();
   670	    session.abort = null;
   671	    if (session.timer !== null) {
   672	      this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   673	      session.timer = null;
   674	    }
   675	    session.tape.abort?.abort();
   676	    session.tape.abort = null;
   677	    if (session.tape.timer !== null) {
   678	      this.clearTimer(session.tape.timer);
   679	      session.tape.timer = null;
   680	    }
   681	    session.seam.abort?.abort();
   682	    session.seam.abort = null;
   683	    if (session.seam.timer !== null) {
   684	      this.clearTimer(session.seam.timer);
   685	      session.seam.timer = null;
   686	    }
   687	    this.closeStream(session);
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  private closeStream(session: Session): void {
   691	    if (session.connectTimer !== null) {
   692	      this.clearTimer(session.connectTimer);
   693	      session.connectTimer = null;
   694	    }
   695	    // The tape this stream delivered is observed up to its last live trade;
   696	    // the prints between that and the next stream's first trade are a hole,
   697	    // which a seam fill closes from REST or which stays open.
   698	    const live = session.live;
   699	    if (live !== null) {
   700	      addInterval(session.observed, live.from, live.last);
   701	      session.live = null;
   702	    }
   703	    const stream = session.stream;
   704	    session.stream = null;
   705	    if (stream === null) return;
   706	    stream.onopen = null;
   707	    stream.onmessage = null;
   708	    stream.onclose = null;
   709	    stream.onerror = null;
   710	    try {
   711	      stream.close();
   712	    } catch {
   713	      void 0;
   714	    }
   715	  }
   716	
   717	  private isCurrent(session: Session): boolean {
   718	    return this.readyState === OPEN && this.session === session && !session.closed;
   719	  }
   720	
   721	  private select(selection: Selection): void {
   722	    this.endSession();
   723	    const perpetual = selection.provider === "BINANCE" && selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL";
   724	    const session: Session = {
   725	      selection,
   726	      binanceSymbol: selection.symbol.replace("-", ""),
   727	      restBase: perpetual ? this.endpoints.perpetualRestBase : this.endpoints.spotRestBase,
   728	      streamBase: perpetual ? this.endpoints.perpetualStreamBase : this.endpoints.spotStreamBase,
   729	      stream: null,
   730	      connectTimer: null,
   731	      streamGeneration: 0,
   732	      degraded: false,
   733	      recovering: false,
   734	      heldClosedBars: new Map(),
   735	      heldFormingBar: null,
   736	      bookLive: false,
   737	      fillInFlight: false,
   738	      awaitingBoundary: null,
   739	      holeRetries: 0,
   740	      streamFailures: 0,
   741	      backoffMs: STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS,
   742	      lastClosedOpen: -1,
   743	      abort: null,
   744	      timer: null,
   745	      closed: false,
   746	      closedBars: new Map(),
   747	      lastFormingBar: null,
   748	      recentTrades: [],
   749	      markers: createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer(MAX_RETAINED_MARKERS, MARKER_BUCKET_MS, MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE),
   750	      pool: createAggressivePool<TradeFrame>({ now: this.now }),
   751	      firstLiveTradeTs: null,
   752	      observed: [],
   753	      live: null,
   754	      unfillable: [],
   755	      calibrationFrom: this.now() - DEFAULT_CALIBRATION_MS,
   756	      tape: { started: false, inFlight: false, done: false, attempts: 0, unavailable: false, backoffMs: STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS, abort: null, timer: null },
   757	      seam: { inFlight: false, gap: null, failures: 0, backoffMs: STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS, abort: null, timer: null, publishWhenSettled: false },
   758	      markerHistorySeq: 0,
   759	    };
   760	    this.session = session;
   761	    this.deliver({ type: "selection", ...identityFields(selection) });
   762	    if (selection.provider !== "BINANCE") {
   763	      this.deliver({ type: "snapshot", ...identityFields(selection), bars: [], trades: [], markers: [] });
   764	      this.deliverHealth(session, "UNAVAILABLE", HEALTH_REASON_NO_DXFEED);
   765	      return;
   766	    }
   767	    void this.bootstrap(session);
   768	  }
   769	
   770	  private deliverHealth(session: Session, recoveryState: "READY" | "RECOVERING" | "UNAVAILABLE", reason: string): void {
   771	    this.deliver({
   772	      type: "health",
   773	      ...identityFields(session.selection),
   774	      recoveryState,
   775	      stale: recoveryState !== "READY",
   776	      reason,
   777	    });
   778	  }
   779	
   780	  private klinesUrl(session: Session, query: Record<string, string | number>): string {
   781	    const path = session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL" ? "/fapi/v1/klines" : "/api/v3/klines";
   782	    const params = new URLSearchParams({ symbol: session.binanceSymbol, interval: "1m" });
   783	    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query)) params.set(key, String(value));
   784	    return `${session.restBase}${path}?${params.toString()}`;
   785	  }
   786	
   787	  private async fetchKlines(session: Session, query: Record<string, string | number>): Promise<KlineRow[]> {
   788	    const controller = new AbortController();
   789	    session.abort = controller;
   790	    const timer = this.setTimer(() => controller.abort(), REST_TIMEOUT_MS);
   791	    try {
   792	      const response = await this.fetchImpl(this.klinesUrl(session, query), { signal: controller.signal });
   793	      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`klines: HTTP ${response.status}`);
   794	      const body: unknown = await response.json();
   795	      if (!Array.isArray(body)) throw new Error("klines: body is not an array");
   796	      const rows: KlineRow[] = [];
   797	      for (const raw of body) {
   798	        const row = parseKlineRow(raw);
   799	        if (row !== null) rows.push(row);
   800	      }
   801	      rows.sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime);
   802	      return rows;
   803	    } finally {
   804	      this.clearTimer(timer);
   805	      if (session.abort === controller) session.abort = null;
   806	    }
   807	  }
   808	
   809	  private scheduleRetry(session: Session, action: () => void): void {
   810	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   811	    if (session.timer !== null) this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   812	    const delay = session.backoffMs;
   813	    session.backoffMs = Math.min(session.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
   814	    session.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
   815	      session.timer = null;
   816	      if (this.isCurrent(session)) action();
   817	    }, delay);
   818	  }
   819	
   820	  private scheduleRetryAt(session: Session, delayMs: number, action: () => void): void {
   821	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   822	    if (session.timer !== null) this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   823	    session.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
   824	      session.timer = null;
   825	      if (this.isCurrent(session)) action();
   826	    }, delayMs);
   827	  }
   828	
   829	  private restFailureReason(session: Session): string {
   830	    return session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL"
   831	      ? HEALTH_REASON_PERPETUAL_UNREACHABLE
   832	      : HEALTH_REASON_REST_FAILED;
   833	  }
   834	
   835	  private async bootstrap(session: Session): Promise<void> {
   836	    let rows: KlineRow[];
   837	    const confirmedBefore = this.now() - CLOSE_CONFIRMATION_MARGIN_MS;
   838	    try {
   839	      rows = await this.fetchKlines(session, { limit: SNAPSHOT_BARS + 1 });
   840	    } catch (error) {
   841	      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   842	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: kline history request failed", error);
   843	      this.deliverHealth(session, "UNAVAILABLE", this.restFailureReason(session));
   844	      this.scheduleRetry(session, () => void this.bootstrap(session));
   845	      return;
   846	    }
   847	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   848	    const now = this.now();
   849	    // Only minutes that had closed before the request went out are final; the
   850	    // minute at the edge may have been served partial and is shown forming
   851	    // until the stream's reconcile confirms it.
   852	    const closedRows = rows.filter((row) => row.closeTime < confirmedBefore).slice(-SNAPSHOT_BARS);
   853	    const forming = rows.find((row) => row.closeTime >= confirmedBefore && row.openTime <= now);
   854	    const identity = identityFields(session.selection);
   855	    this.deliver({
   856	      type: "snapshot",
   857	      ...identity,
   858	      bars: closedRows.map((row) => barFrame(session.selection, row, true, true)),
   859	      trades: [],
   860	      markers: [],
   861	      vpin_status: "DISABLED",
   862	    });
   863	    session.lastClosedOpen = closedRows.length > 0 ? closedRows[closedRows.length - 1].openTime : -1;
   864	    session.closedBars = new Map(closedRows.map((row) => [row.openTime, { ...row, reconstructed: true }]));
   865	    session.lastFormingBar = null;
   866	    session.heldClosedBars.clear();
   867	    session.heldFormingBar = null;
   868	    session.recovering = false;
   869	    session.fillInFlight = false;
   870	    session.awaitingBoundary = null;
   871	    session.holeRetries = 0;
   872	    session.degraded = false;
   873	    session.bookLive = false;
   874	    this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", this.readyReason(session));
   875	    this.deliver({ type: "book", ...identity, status: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: BOOK_REASON_STREAM_CONNECTING });
   876	    if (forming !== undefined) this.deliverFormingBar(session, forming);
   877	    session.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
   878	    this.openStream(session);
   879	    this.startTapeBootstrap(session);
   880	  }

import type { MarkerFrame } from "../../types/frames";
import { markerMagnitude, type WhaleTradeStat } from "../indicators/whale-detector/whaleTrades";

export const DEFAULT_MARKER_COVERAGE_BUCKET_MS = 60_000;
export const DEFAULT_MAX_MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE = 4;
const HEAP_COMPACTION_RATIO = 4;

function lowerMagnitudePriority(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean {
  const candidateMagnitude = markerMagnitude(candidate);
  const incumbentMagnitude = markerMagnitude(incumbent);
  if (candidateMagnitude !== incumbentMagnitude) return candidateMagnitude < incumbentMagnitude;
  if (candidate.ts_exchange !== incumbent.ts_exchange) return candidate.ts_exchange < incumbent.ts_exchange;
  return candidate.trade_id < incumbent.trade_id;
}

function olderFirst(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean {
  return candidate.ts_exchange !== incumbent.ts_exchange
    ? candidate.ts_exchange < incumbent.ts_exchange
    : candidate.trade_id < incumbent.trade_id;
}

function snapshotOrder(a: MarkerFrame, b: MarkerFrame): number {
  return a.ts_exchange - b.ts_exchange || (a.trade_id < b.trade_id ? -1 : a.trade_id > b.trade_id ? 1 : 0);
}

function higherMagnitudeFirst(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean {
  return lowerMagnitudePriority(incumbent, candidate);
}

function statOrder(left: WhaleTradeStat, right: WhaleTradeStat): number {
  return snapshotOrder(left.marker, right.marker);
}

type LowerThan = (candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame) => boolean;

function heapSwap(heap: MarkerFrame[], left: number, right: number): void {
  const tmp = heap[left];
  heap[left] = heap[right];
  heap[right] = tmp;
}

function heapPush(heap: MarkerFrame[], value: MarkerFrame, lower: LowerThan): void {
  heap.push(value);
  let index = heap.length - 1;
  while (index > 0) {
    const parent = Math.floor((index - 1) / 2);
    if (!lower(heap[index], heap[parent])) break;
    heapSwap(heap, index, parent);
    index = parent;
  }
}

function heapSiftDown(heap: MarkerFrame[], index: number, lower: LowerThan): void {
  while (true) {
    const left = index * 2 + 1;
    const right = left + 1;
    let lowest = index;
    if (left < heap.length && lower(heap[left], heap[lowest])) lowest = left;
    if (right < heap.length && lower(heap[right], heap[lowest])) lowest = right;
    if (lowest === index) return;
    heapSwap(heap, index, lowest);
    index = lowest;
  }
}

function heapPopRoot(heap: MarkerFrame[], lower: LowerThan): MarkerFrame | undefined {
  const root = heap[0];
  const last = heap.pop();
  if (heap.length > 0 && last !== undefined) {
    heap[0] = last;
    heapSiftDown(heap, 0, lower);
  }
  return root;
}

export interface TopMagnitudeSelection {
  stats: WhaleTradeStat[];
  resolvedThreshold: number | null;
}

export interface MarkerSelectionView {
  readonly version: number;
  size(): number;
  snapshot(): MarkerFrame[];
  topByMagnitude(limit: number): TopMagnitudeSelection;
  atLeastMagnitude(minMagnitude: number, maxMagnitude?: number | null): WhaleTradeStat[];
}

export interface MarkerRetentionBuffer extends MarkerSelectionView {
  insertAll(markers: readonly MarkerFrame[]): boolean;
}

export function createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer(
  maxMarkers: number,
  bucketMs: number = DEFAULT_MARKER_COVERAGE_BUCKET_MS,
  maxPerBucketSide: number = DEFAULT_MAX_MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE,
): MarkerRetentionBuffer {
  if (!Number.isInteger(maxMarkers) || maxMarkers <= 0) {
    throw new Error("createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer: maxMarkers must be a positive integer");
  }
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(bucketMs) || bucketMs <= 0) {
    throw new Error("createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer: bucketMs must be a positive safe integer");
  }
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxPerBucketSide) || maxPerBucketSide <= 0) {
    throw new Error("createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer: maxPerBucketSide must be a positive safe integer");
  }

  const byId = new Map<string, MarkerFrame>();
  const idsByBucketSide = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  let timeHeap: MarkerFrame[] = [];
  let magnitudeHeap: MarkerFrame[] = [];
  let version = 0;

  function bucketKey(marker: MarkerFrame): string {
    return `${Math.floor(marker.ts_exchange / bucketMs)}:${marker.side}`;
  }

  function removeFromBucket(marker: MarkerFrame): void {
    byId.delete(marker.trade_id);
    const key = bucketKey(marker);
    const ids = idsByBucketSide.get(key);
    if (!ids) return;
    ids.delete(marker.trade_id);
    if (ids.size === 0) idsByBucketSide.delete(key);
  }

  function popOldestLive(): MarkerFrame | null {
    let popped = heapPopRoot(timeHeap, olderFirst);
    while (popped !== undefined) {
      if (byId.get(popped.trade_id) === popped) return popped;
      popped = heapPopRoot(timeHeap, olderFirst);
    }
    return null;
  }

  function compactTimeHeapIfNeeded(): void {
    if (timeHeap.length <= HEAP_COMPACTION_RATIO * maxMarkers) return;
    const rebuilt: MarkerFrame[] = [];
    for (const marker of byId.values()) heapPush(rebuilt, marker, olderFirst);
    timeHeap = rebuilt;
  }

  function compactMagnitudeHeapIfNeeded(): void {
    if (magnitudeHeap.length <= HEAP_COMPACTION_RATIO * Math.max(byId.size, 1)) return;
    const rebuilt: MarkerFrame[] = [];
    for (const marker of byId.values()) heapPush(rebuilt, marker, higherMagnitudeFirst);
    magnitudeHeap = rebuilt;
  }

  function topByMagnitude(limit: number): TopMagnitudeSelection {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 0) {
      throw new Error("createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer: topByMagnitude limit must be a non-negative safe integer");
    }
    const taken: MarkerFrame[] = [];
    while (taken.length < limit) {
      const root = heapPopRoot(magnitudeHeap, higherMagnitudeFirst);
      if (root === undefined) break;
      if (byId.get(root.trade_id) === root) taken.push(root);
    }
    for (const marker of taken) heapPush(magnitudeHeap, marker, higherMagnitudeFirst);
    const stats = taken.map((marker) => ({ marker, magnitude: markerMagnitude(marker) }));
    const resolvedThreshold = stats.length === 0 ? null : stats[stats.length - 1].magnitude;
    stats.sort(statOrder);
    return { stats, resolvedThreshold };
  }

  function atLeastMagnitude(minMagnitude: number, maxMagnitude: number | null = null): WhaleTradeStat[] {
    if (!Number.isFinite(minMagnitude) || minMagnitude <= 0) {
      throw new Error("createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer: atLeastMagnitude minMagnitude must be a positive finite number");
    }
    if (maxMagnitude !== null && (!Number.isFinite(maxMagnitude) || maxMagnitude < minMagnitude)) {
      throw new Error("createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer: atLeastMagnitude maxMagnitude must be a finite number no smaller than minMagnitude");
    }
    const stats: WhaleTradeStat[] = [];
    const pending = magnitudeHeap.length === 0 ? [] : [0];
    while (pending.length > 0) {
      const index = pending.pop()!;
      const marker = magnitudeHeap[index];
      const magnitude = markerMagnitude(marker);
      // This is a max-heap. Once a node is below the floor, every descendant
      // is below it too, so the entire branch can be skipped. Lazy-deleted
      // nodes still participate in that ordering but are never returned.
      if (magnitude < minMagnitude) continue;
      if (byId.get(marker.trade_id) === marker && (maxMagnitude === null || magnitude <= maxMagnitude)) {
        stats.push({ marker, magnitude });
      }
      const left = index * 2 + 1;
      if (left < magnitudeHeap.length) pending.push(left);
      const right = left + 1;
      if (right < magnitudeHeap.length) pending.push(right);
    }
    stats.sort(statOrder);
    return stats;
  }

  function insertOne(marker: MarkerFrame): boolean {
    if (byId.has(marker.trade_id)) return false;
    const key = bucketKey(marker);
    const ids = idsByBucketSide.get(key) ?? new Set<string>();
    if (ids.size >= maxPerBucketSide) {
      let lowest: MarkerFrame | null = null;
      for (const id of ids) {
        const existing = byId.get(id);
        if (existing && (lowest === null || lowerMagnitudePriority(existing, lowest))) lowest = existing;
      }
      if (lowest !== null && lowerMagnitudePriority(marker, lowest)) return false;
      if (lowest !== null) removeFromBucket(lowest);
    }
    byId.set(marker.trade_id, marker);
    const nextIds = idsByBucketSide.get(key) ?? new Set<string>();
    nextIds.add(marker.trade_id);
    idsByBucketSide.set(key, nextIds);
    heapPush(timeHeap, marker, olderFirst);
    heapPush(magnitudeHeap, marker, higherMagnitudeFirst);
    if (byId.size > maxMarkers) {
      const oldest = popOldestLive();
      if (oldest !== null) removeFromBucket(oldest);
    }
    compactTimeHeapIfNeeded();
    compactMagnitudeHeapIfNeeded();
    return true;
  }

  return {
    get version() { return version; },
    size() { return byId.size; },
    insertAll(markers) {
      let changed = false;
      for (const marker of markers) if (insertOne(marker)) changed = true;
      if (changed) version += 1;

exec

  const SEAM_SIZE = "5.00000";
  /** Rows the mirror holds after the recording: two inside the seam, one at the live trade's time and one after it. */
  function seamRows(liveFrom: number): TradeFrame[] {
    return [NEWEST_TS + 1_000, NEWEST_TS + 2_000, liveFrom, liveFrom + 500].map((ts, index) => ({
      type: "trade", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", ts_exchange: ts, price: 79_530, size: Number(SEAM_SIZE), side: "buy", trade_id: `a${LAST_ID + 1 + index}`,
    }));
  }

  it("claims nothing across the seam when the stream connects after the bootstrap, then fills the seam from REST and claims the whole stretch", async () => {
    const liveFrom = NEWEST_TS + 3_000;
    const tape = [...recorded, ...seamRows(liveFrom)];
    let release: (() => void) | null = null;
    const h = harness((url) => {
      if (!url.includes("startTime")) return Promise.resolve(jsonResponse(recordedPage(url, recorded)));
      return new Promise<Response>((resolve) => {
        release = () => resolve(jsonResponse(recordedPage(url, tape)));
      });
    });
    await openAndSelect(h);

    expect(first.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(NOW_MS);
    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(1, OLDEST_TS, NOW_MS, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(2, OLDEST_TS, NEWEST_TS + 1, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });

    // The stream connects late; its first trade opens the live span and starts the seam fill.
    h.stream().open();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    h.frames.length = 0;
    h.stream().message(liveTrade(9_000_000_001, liveFrom, SEAM_SIZE, false));
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    expect(String(h.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.at(-1)![0])).toContain(`startTime=${NEWEST_TS + 1}`);
    // Until the fill lands, the hole between the newest page and the first
    // live trade is inside no claim (the live print's size has moved the
    // floor, so these ask well above it).
    const aboveFloor = 1;
    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(3, OLDEST_TS, liveFrom + 1_000, aboveFloor))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(4, liveFrom, liveFrom + 1_000, aboveFloor))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
    expect(h.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(0);

    release!();
    await settle();
    const frames = h.parsed();
    expect(frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
    const snapshot = frames.find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")!;
    if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(OLDEST_TS);
    // The seam's own prints are in the pool; the rows at and after the live trade were the stream's and are not.
    const ids = snapshot.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id);
    expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 1}`);
    expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 2}`);
    expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 3}`);
    expect(ids).toContain("9000000001");
    const whole = await askHistory(h, historyRequest(5, OLDEST_TS, liveFrom + 1_000, aboveFloor));
    expect(whole).toMatchObject({ complete: true, reason: null });
    expect(whole!.trades.map((trade) => trade.trade_id).slice(-3)).toEqual([`a${LAST_ID + 1}`, `a${LAST_ID + 2}`, "9000000001"]);
  });

      // Three failed seam reads with backoff: the hole stays open, every claim stops at it.
      let delay = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;

        await settle();

      h.stream().drop();
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS);
      h.stream().open();
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);

      seamFails = false;
      h.frames.length = 0;

    } finally {
      warn.mockRestore();
    }
  });

import type {
  AggressivePrint,
  AggressiveTape,
} from "../indicators/aggressive-orders/aggressiveTape";

/**
 * The terminal's own L1 print store, as an indicator sees it.
 *
 * `AggressiveTape` holds every print of the live pair -- fed the moment it lands
 * -- plus whatever history the server could serve for before this browser
 * connected (a bounded, **floor-filtered** pool, not the raw tape). It is the
 * same store the whale detector and Aggressive Order Flow read.
 *
 * Because history is served above a size floor and the live tape is complete,
 * a window is either covered by every print (`"all"`), covered only by prints
 * above that floor (`"thin"`), or not covered at all (`"none"`). An indicator
 * that prices volume has to say which one it is looking at rather than assume.
 */
export type TickCoverage = "all" | "thin" | "none";

export interface IndicatorTicks {
  /** Every print the store still holds, oldest first. */
  readonly prints: readonly AggressivePrint[];
  /** The instant complete live recording began, or null when nothing is live yet. */
  readonly liveSince: number | null;
  /** How the store covers `[fromTs, toTs)`. */
  coverageOf(fromTs: number, toTs: number): TickCoverage;
}

/** The index of the first print at or after `ts`, or `prints.length`. */
export function firstPrintAtOrAfter(
  prints: readonly AggressivePrint[],
  ts: number,
): number {
  let low = 0;
  let high = prints.length;
  while (low < high) {
    const middle = (low + high) >> 1;
    if (prints[middle].ts < ts) low = middle + 1;
    else high = middle;
  }
  return low;
}

export function sumPrintsIn(
  prints: readonly AggressivePrint[],
  fromTs: number,
  toTs: number,
): { volume: number; priceVolume: number; priceSquaredVolume: number } {
  let volume = 0;
  let priceVolume = 0;
  let priceSquaredVolume = 0;
  for (let index = firstPrintAtOrAfter(prints, fromTs); index < prints.length; index += 1) {
    const print = prints[index];
    if (print.ts >= toTs) break;
    const { price, size } = print;
    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(size) || size <= 0) continue;
    volume += size;
    priceVolume += price * size;
    priceSquaredVolume += price * price * size;
  }
  return { volume, priceVolume, priceSquaredVolume };
}

function coversWindow(
  intervals: readonly { from: number; to: number }[],
  fromTs: number,
  toTs: number,
): boolean {
  return intervals.some(
    (interval) => interval.from <= fromTs && interval.to >= toTs,
  );
}

export function indicatorTicksOf(tape: AggressiveTape | null): IndicatorTicks | null {
  if (tape === null) return null;
  return {
    get prints(): readonly AggressivePrint[] {
      return tape.prints;
    },
    get liveSince(): number | null {
      return tape.liveSince;
    },
    coverageOf(fromTs: number, toTs: number): TickCoverage {
      // Every print from the moment live recording began; the window's end may
      // be in the future while the bar is still forming, which is still covered.
      // An earlier live stretch (before a reconnect) covers what lies inside it.
      // Either is complete only past the newest live print the store's size cap
      // dropped: a window it reached lost prints, so it reads as thin.
      const liveFrom = tape.liveSince;
      const live =
        (liveFrom !== null && fromTs >= liveFrom) ||
        tape.liveSpans.some((span) => span.from <= fromTs && span.to >= toTs);
      if (live) return fromTs > tape.liveDroppedThrough ? "all" : "thin";
      // A floor of zero is the server saying it served this stretch in full.
      if (coversWindow(tape.historyIntervalsAt(0), fromTs, toTs)) return "all";
      if (coversWindow(tape.historyIntervalsAt(Infinity), fromTs, toTs))
        return "thin";
      return "none";
    },
  };
}

  1143	  private routeStreamMessage(session: Session, parsed: unknown): void {
  1144	    if (!isRecord(parsed)) return;
  1145	    const payload = isRecord(parsed.data) && typeof parsed.stream === "string" ? parsed.data : parsed;
  1146	    if (payload.e === "kline") {
  1147	      const bar = parseKlineEvent(payload.k);
  1148	      if (bar === null) return;
  1149	      if (bar.closed && bar.openTime <= session.lastClosedOpen) return;
  1150	      if (session.recovering) {
  1151	        if (bar.closed && !session.fillInFlight && session.awaitingBoundary === bar.openTime && bar.openTime === session.lastClosedOpen + BAR_INTERVAL_MS) {
  1152	          // The minute the fill could not yet confirm: the stream's close is final.
  1153	          if (session.timer !== null) {
  1154	            this.clearTimer(session.timer);
  1155	            session.timer = null;
  1156	          }

  1161	            this.scheduleRetry(session, () => {
  1162	              if (this.recoveryIsLive(session, session.streamGeneration)) void this.recoverAfterGap(session, session.streamGeneration);
  1163	            });
  1164	          }
  1165	          return;
  1166	        }
  1167	        if (bar.closed) {
  1168	          session.heldClosedBars.set(bar.openTime, bar);
  1169	          // Bounded: a dropped old close is still a closed minute on the
  1170	          // exchange, and the paginated REST read walks up to the earliest
  1171	          // retained one, so nothing is lost.
  1172	          if (session.heldClosedBars.size > MAX_HELD_CLOSED_BARS) {

  1174	            if (earliest !== undefined) session.heldClosedBars.delete(earliest);
  1175	          }
  1176	        } else session.heldFormingBar = bar;
  1177	        return;
  1178	      }
  1179	      if (bar.closed) this.deliverClosedBar(session, bar, false);
  1180	      else this.deliverFormingBar(session, bar);
  1181	      return;

  1185	      const size = finiteNumber(payload.q);
  1470	
  1471	  /**
  1472	   * One validated page into the retention and the pool, as the runtime's
  1473	   * warm-up seeds a storage chunk: rows from the calibration window (the
  1474	   * newest fifteen minutes at the bootstrap's start) calibrate the floor
  1475	   * (`calibrate: true`, every size counted), older rows are admitted at the
  1476	   * floor. Rows at or after `cutoff` (the first live trade, for the
  1477	   * bootstrap) are skipped; when a page reached past that point the observed
  1478	   * stretch runs up to it.
  1479	   */
  1480	  private seedTape(session: Session, rows: readonly AggTradeRow[], cutoff: number | null): { kept: number; oldestTs: number | null; newestTs: number | null } {
  1481	    const calibration: TradeFrame[] = [];
  1482	    const older: TradeFrame[] = [];
  1483	    let oldestTs = Infinity;
  1484	    let newestTs = -Infinity;
  1485	    let reachedLive = false;
  1486	    for (const row of rows) {
  1487	      if (cutoff !== null && row.T >= cutoff) {
  1488	        reachedLive = true;
  1489	        continue;
  1490	      }
  1491	      const trade = aggTradeFrame(session.selection, row);
  1492	      if (row.T < oldestTs) oldestTs = row.T;
  1493	      if (row.T > newestTs) newestTs = row.T;
  1494	      (row.T >= session.calibrationFrom ? calibration : older).push(trade);
  1495	      this.offerMarker(session, trade, false);
  1496	    }
  1497	    if (oldestTs === Infinity) return { kept: 0, oldestTs: null, newestTs: null };
  1498	    if (calibration.length > 0) session.pool.seed(calibration, { fromMs: oldestTs, calibrate: true });
  1499	    if (older.length > 0) session.pool.seed(older, { fromMs: oldestTs });
  1500	    return { kept: calibration.length + older.length, oldestTs, newestTs: reachedLive && cutoff !== null ? Math.max(newestTs, cutoff - 1) : newestTs };
  1501	  }
  1502	
  1503	  /** The newest hole between two observed stretches (the open stream span counts) that has not been given up on. */
  1504	  private nextSeamGap(session: Session): TapeInterval | null {
  1505	    const intervals = this.coverageIntervals(session);
  1506	    for (let i = intervals.length - 1; i >= 1; i -= 1) {
  1507	      const gap = { from: intervals[i - 1].to, to: intervals[i].from };
  1508	      if (!session.unfillable.some((open) => open.from === gap.from && open.to === gap.to)) return gap;
  1509	    }
  1510	    return null;
  1511	  }
  1512	
  1513	  /**
  1514	   * Closes the holes the tape has: between the bootstrap's newest row and the
  1515	   * stream's first trade, or between one stream's last trade and the next
  1516	   * stream's first. Kline recovery repairs bars, not trades, so the prints of
  1517	   * such a hole are read from REST (aggTrades from the hole's start, then by
  1518	   * id) within a bounded budget; a hole that cannot be closed stays a hole and
  1519	   * is never inside a completeness claim. One fill runs at a time; when
  1520	   * nothing is left to fill and the bootstrap's snapshot is waiting, it goes
  1521	   * out.
  1522	   */
  1523	  private fillNextSeam(session: Session): void {
  1524	    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || session.seam.inFlight || session.seam.timer !== null) return;
  1525	    const gap = this.nextSeamGap(session);
  1526	    if (gap === null) {
  1527	      if (session.seam.publishWhenSettled) this.publishTapeState(session);
  1528	      return;
  1529	    }
  1530	    void this.fillSeam(session, gap);
  1531	  }
  1532	
  1533	  private async fillSeam(session: Session, gap: TapeInterval): Promise<void> {
  1534	    const seam = session.seam;
  1535	    seam.inFlight = true;
  1536	    seam.gap = gap;
  1537	    let closed = false;
  1538	    try {
  1539	      let fromId: number | null = null;
  1540	      for (let pages = 0; pages < MAX_SEAM_FILL_PAGES; pages += 1) {
  1541	        const rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? { startTime: gap.from + 1 } : { fromId }, seam);
  1542	        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
  1543	        const inside = rows.filter((row) => row.T < gap.to);
  1544	        if (inside.length > 0) this.seedTape(session, inside, null);
  1545	        // The hole is closed when a row past its end was read, or the page
  1546	        // was short (nothing newer existed on the exchange at the read).
  1547	        if (inside.length < rows.length || rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) {
  1548	          closed = true;
  1549	          break;
  1550	        }
  1551	        fromId = rows[rows.length - 1].a + 1;
  1552	      }
  1553	    } catch (error) {
  1554	      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
  1555	      seam.inFlight = false;
  1556	      seam.gap = null;
  1557	      seam.failures += 1;
  1558	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam fill failed", error);
  1559	      if (seam.failures < SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP) {
  1560	        const delay = seam.backoffMs;
  1561	        seam.backoffMs = Math.min(seam.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
  1562	        seam.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
  1563	          seam.timer = null;
  1564	          this.fillNextSeam(session);
  1565	        }, delay);
  1566	        return;
  1567	      }
  1568	      seam.failures = 0;
  1569	      seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
  1570	      session.unfillable.push(gap);
  1571	      this.fillNextSeam(session);
  1572	      return;
  1573	    }
  1574	    seam.inFlight = false;
  1575	    seam.gap = null;
  1576	    seam.failures = 0;
  1577	    seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
  1578	    if (closed) {
  1579	      addInterval(session.observed, gap.from, gap.to);
  1580	      // Coverage grew: the snapshot says so.
  1581	      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
  1582	    } else {
  1583	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam is wider than a fill may walk; it stays open");
  1584	      session.unfillable.push(gap);
  1585	    }
  1586	    this.fillNextSeam(session);
  1587	  }

/** A deterministic PRNG so the pairwise checks are the same run after run. */
function lcg(seed: number): () => number {
  let state = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    state = (Math.imul(state, 1_664_525) + 1_013_904_223) >>> 0;
    return state / 0x1_0000_0000;
  };
}

describe("tape parity: the recorded tape", () => {
  it("is 10,000 trades of one pair with unique ids, in time order", () => {
    expect(trades).toHaveLength(10_000);
    expect(new Set(trades.map((trade) => trade.trade_id)).size).toBe(10_000);

  absorb(trades: readonly TradeFrame[]): void {
    // A (re)connect: the stretch recorded so far stays complete up to its last live print.
    if (this._liveSince !== null && this._lastLiveTs > this._liveSince) {
      this._liveSpans.push({ from: this._liveSince, to: this._lastLiveTs });
      if (this._liveSpans.length > MAX_LIVE_SPANS) this._liveSpans.splice(0, this._liveSpans.length - MAX_LIVE_SPANS);
    }
    this._liveSince = this._now();
    let taken = 0;
    for (const trade of trades) {
      if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
      const print = toPrint(trade);
      this._push(print);
      this._fold(print);
      taken += 1;
    }
    this._trim();
    if (taken > 0 || this._prints.length === 0) this._notify();
  }

  add(trade: TradeFrame): void {
    if (this._liveSince === null) this._liveSince = this._now();
    if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) return;
    const print = toPrint(trade);
    this._push(print);
    this._fold(print);
    this._trim();
    this._notify();
  }

  addHistory(trades: readonly TradeFrame[], thinBeforeMs = -Infinity): number {
    let taken = 0;
    for (const trade of trades) {
      if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
      this._push(toPrint(trade, true), false);
      taken += 1;
    }
    if (taken === 0) return 0;
    this._prints.sort(comparePrints);
    if (thinBeforeMs > -Infinity) this._thin(thinBeforeMs);
    this._trim();
    this._autoFloorCount = -1;
    this._generation += 1;
    this._historyVersion += 1;
    this._tallyDirty = true;
    this._settleUntil = this._now() + HISTORY_SETTLE_MS;
    this._notify();
    return taken;
  }

  1392	  /**
  1393	   * The runtime seeds markers and the pool from storage; the browser reads
  1394	   * recent aggregate trades from the mirror, newest page first by id, within a
  1395	   * bounded page budget, and feeds them through the same retention and pool.
  1396	   * Rows at or after the first live trade are the stream's and are skipped.
  1397	   * The pages read are one id-contiguous stretch, recorded as observed; a
  1398	   * page that fails validation or transport after others succeeded ends the
  1399	   * walk with what was read; a first page that fails is retried with backoff,
  1400	   * and after three such failures the READY health frame says the tape
  1401	   * history is missing. The snapshot goes out once the seam to the stream is
  1402	   * settled (closed by a fill, or left open), or at once with no stream.
  1403	   */
  1404	  private async bootstrapTape(session: Session): Promise<void> {

  if (fromId !== null && rows.length > 0 && rows[0].a !== fromId) throw new Error("aggTrades: page does not start at fromId");
  return rows;
}

/** Merges a closed stretch into the list, joining stretches that overlap or touch (adjacent milliseconds). */
function addInterval(intervals: TapeInterval[], from: number, to: number): void {
  if (!(to >= from)) return;
  let merged: TapeInterval = { from, to };
  const apart: TapeInterval[] = [];
  for (const interval of intervals) {
    if (interval.to + 1 < merged.from || interval.from > merged.to + 1) apart.push(interval);
    else merged = { from: Math.min(interval.from, merged.from), to: Math.max(interval.to, merged.to) };
  }
  apart.push(merged);
  apart.sort((a, b) => a.from - b.from);
  intervals.splice(0, intervals.length, ...apart);
}

/**
 * A bootstrap print as a trade frame. Aggregate trade ids are a separate
 * sequence from the stream's trade ids, so they carry an `a` prefix: the two
 * can never be mistaken for one another, and nothing is counted twice.
 */
function aggTradeFrame(selection: Selection, row: AggTradeRow): TradeFrame {

1. **CRITICAL** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:695–701](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:695), also lines 1568–1572: **Reconnect holes never reach the chart’s tick coverage state.** The transport closes its internal live interval, but sends only health frames. `useMarketData` does not close the tick store’s live span on health changes, so `ticks.coverageOf` continues returning `"all"` across missing trades—even when `aggressive_history` correctly returns `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE`. A later snapshot cannot reliably repair this: `tape.absorb` preserves the previous span through the newest post-reconnect trade. **Fix:** propagate stream boundaries to the tick store and end its live span immediately on disconnect. Add an integration test through `useMarketData` asserting coverage across an unfilled reconnect hole remains unavailable.

2. **WARNING** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:1541–1548](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1541): **Seam fills omit unseen trades at boundary timestamps.** Multiple Binance trades can share one millisecond. If the stream disconnects after trade A at timestamp T, unseen trade B at T is excluded by `startTime: gap.from + 1`. Nevertheless, the fill merges the entire interval as complete. The upper boundary has the corresponding problem when earlier trades share the first live trade’s timestamp. **Fix:** reconcile boundaries using trade identities, including aggregate-to-individual fill ranges, or conservatively exclude unverified boundary milliseconds from coverage. Pin both cases with hand-built trades sharing timestamps.

3. **WARNING** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:1427–1430](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1427), also lines 1197–1207: **Bootstrap deduplication depends on arrival order.** A REST page can be seeded while `firstLiveTradeTs` is still null. If the first delayed stream event then overlaps that page, setting the cutoff does not remove previously seeded aggregates. Their `a…` identifiers differ from live trade identifiers, so both representations survive, inflating prints and potentially markers. The existing test covers only live-before-REST ordering. **Fix:** establish the cutoff before committing bootstrap data, or reconcile already seeded data when the first live trade arrives. Test REST-before-live overlap as well.

4. **WARNING** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:1570–1584](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1570), also lines 695–701: **Coverage bookkeeping grows without a bound.** Every unfillable reconnect can retain another `observed` interval and `unfillable` entry indefinitely. Both remain after the pool’s four-hour retention expires, increasing memory and repeated interval scans/copies. **Fix:** prune intervals and abandoned gaps outside the pool’s retention window, preserving any active fill boundary. Add a repeated-reconnect test demonstrating bounded state.

The requested diff was reviewed. Test execution was attempted in the branch worktree, but all four suites failed before collecting tests because Vitest could not create temporary directories in the read-only environment.

1. **CRITICAL** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:695–701](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:695), also lines 1568–1572: **Reconnect holes never reach the chart’s tick coverage state.** The transport closes its internal live interval, but sends only health frames. `useMarketData` does not close the tick store’s live span on health changes, so `ticks.coverageOf` continues returning `"all"` across missing trades—even when `aggressive_history` correctly returns `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE`. A later snapshot cannot reliably repair this: `tape.absorb` preserves the previous span through the newest post-reconnect trade. **Fix:** propagate stream boundaries to the tick store and end its live span immediately on disconnect. Add an integration test through `useMarketData` asserting coverage across an unfilled reconnect hole remains unavailable.

2. **WARNING** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:1541–1548](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1541): **Seam fills omit unseen trades at boundary timestamps.** Multiple Binance trades can share one millisecond. If the stream disconnects after trade A at timestamp T, unseen trade B at T is excluded by `startTime: gap.from + 1`. Nevertheless, the fill merges the entire interval as complete. The upper boundary has the corresponding problem when earlier trades share the first live trade’s timestamp. **Fix:** reconcile boundaries using trade identities, including aggregate-to-individual fill ranges, or conservatively exclude unverified boundary milliseconds from coverage. Pin both cases with hand-built trades sharing timestamps.

3. **WARNING** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:1427–1430](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1427), also lines 1197–1207: **Bootstrap deduplication depends on arrival order.** A REST page can be seeded while `firstLiveTradeTs` is still null. If the first delayed stream event then overlaps that page, setting the cutoff does not remove previously seeded aggregates. Their `a…` identifiers differ from live trade identifiers, so both representations survive, inflating prints and potentially markers. The existing test covers only live-before-REST ordering. **Fix:** establish the cutoff before committing bootstrap data, or reconcile already seeded data when the first live trade arrives. Test REST-before-live overlap as well.

4. **WARNING** — [browserBinanceTransport.ts:1570–1584](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/browser-direct-l1/apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1570), also lines 695–701: **Coverage bookkeeping grows without a bound.** Every unfillable reconnect can retain another `observed` interval and `unfillable` entry indefinitely. Both remain after the pool’s four-hour retention expires, increasing memory and repeated interval scans/copies. **Fix:** prune intervals and abandoned gaps outside the pool’s retention window, preserving any active fill boundary. Add a repeated-reconnect test demonstrating bounded state.
